Compare that against the \"required\" figure printed by\n23\t * docs/research/sim/ge_cost_model.py for the same (K, L) and wire rate.\n24\t */\n25\t\n26\tconst HEADER = 13;\n27\t\n28\t// ---------------------------------------------------------------- prng (mulberry32)\n29\tfunction prng(seed) {\n30\t  let a = seed >>> 0;\n31\t  return () => {\n32\t    a = (a + 0x6d2b79f5) >>> 0;\n33\t    let t = a;\n34\t    t = Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 15), t | 1);\n35\t    t ^= t + Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 7), t | 61);\n36\t    return ((t ^ (t >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296;\n37\t  };\n38\t}\n39\t\n40\t/** Harmonic Pr(d) ∝ 1/d truncated at `cap` — D25. */\n41\tfunction degreeSampler(K, cap, rnd) {\n42\t  const hi = Math.min(cap ?? }\n52\t    return lo + 1;\n53\t  };\n54\t}\n55\t\n56\t// ---------------------------------------------------------------- the decode\n57\texport function run(K, L, { cap = 64, seed = 0xC0FFEE } = {}) {\n58\t  const MASKW = Math.ceil(K / 32);\n59\t  const PAYW = Math.ceil(L / 4);\n60\t  const ROWB = MASKW * 4 + PAYW * 4;      // bytes touched per row operation\n61\t\n62\t  // Pivot store: index = pivot bit position.\n63\t  const pivMask = new Array(K).fill(null);\n64\t  const pivPay = new Array(K).fill(null);\n65\t\n66\t  const rnd = prng(seed);\n67\t  const degree = degreeSampler(K, cap, rnd);\n68\t  const idx = new Int32Array(K);\n69\t\n70\t  let rank = 0, packets = 0, rowOps = 0;\n71\t  const mask = new Uint32Array(MASKW);\n72\t  const pay = new Uint32Array(PAYW);\n73\t\n74\t  const t0 = performance.now();\n75\t\n76\t  while (rank < K) {\n77\t    // --- build one encoded packet\n78\t    mask.fill(0);\n79\t    for (let i = 0; i < PAYW; i++) pay[i] = (rnd() * 4294967296) >>> 0;\n80\t    const d = degree();\n81\t    // sample d distinct indices (partial Fisher–Yates over a reused scratch)\n82\t    for (let i = 0; i < K; i++) idx[i] = i;\n83\t    for (let i = 0; Runs in Node or a browser.\n * This is the cheapest possible check of a decision the whole block layer rests on.\n *\n *   node spike/ge-bench.mjs              # default sweep\n *   node spike/ge-bench.mjs 768 256      # one (K, L)\n *\n * In a browser: import it and call run(K, L) — same code path, and the browser\n * number is the one that matters. Node and Chrome share V8, but a phone is the\n * target; expect roughly 3–5x slower than a desktop.\n *\n * WHAT IT MEASURES\n * It performs a complete GF(2) Gaussian elimination decode of one block — the\n * real inner loop, not a synthetic XOR microbenchmark — and reports the sustained\n * XOR throughput achieved. Compare that against the \"required\" figure printed by\n * docs/research/sim/ge_cost_model.py for the same (K, L) and wire rate.\n */\n\nconst HEADER = 13;\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------- prng (mulberry32)\nfunction prng(seed) {\n  let a = seed >>> 0;\n  return () => {\n    a = (a + 0x6d2b79f5) >>> 0;\n    let t = a;\n    t = Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 15), t | 1);\n    t ^= t + Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 7), t | 61);\n    return ((t ^ (t >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296;\n  };\n}\n\n/** Harmonic Pr(d) ∝ 1/d truncated at `cap` — D25. */\nfunction degreeSampler(K, cap, rnd) {\n  const hi = Math.min(cap ?? K, K);\n  const cum = new Float64Array(hi);\n  let total = 0;\n  for (let d = 1; d <= hi; d++) total += 1 / d;\n  let acc = 0;\n  for (let d = 1; d <= hi; d++) { acc += 1 / d / total; cum[d - 1] = acc; }\n  return () => {\n    const r = rnd();\n    let lo = 0, high = hi - 1;\n    while (lo < high) { const mid = (lo + high) >> 1; if (cum[mid] < r) lo = mid + 1; else high = mid; }\n    return lo + 1;\n  };\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------- the decode\nexport function run(K, L, { cap = 64, seed = 0xC0FFEE } = {}) {\n  const MASKW = Math.ceil(K / 32);\n  const PAYW = Math.ceil(L / 4);\n  const ROWB = MASKW * 4 + PAYW * 4;      // bytes touched per row operation\n\n  // Pivot store: index = pivot bit position.\n  const pivMask = new Array(K).fill(null);\n  const pivPay = new Array(K).fill(null);\n\n  const rnd = prng(seed);\n  const degree = degreeSampler(K, cap, rnd);\n  const idx = new Int32Array(K);\n\n  let rank = 0, packets = 0, rowOps = 0;\n  const mask = new Uint32Array(MASKW);\n  const pay = new Uint32Array(PAYW);\n\n  const t0 = performance.now();\n\n  while (rank < K) {\n    // --- build one encoded packet\n    mask.fill(0);\n    for (let i = 0; i < PAYW; i++) pay[i] = (rnd() * 4294967296) >>> 0;\n    const d = degree();\n    // sample d distinct indices (partial Fisher–Yates over a reused scratch)\n    for (let i = 0; i < K; i++) idx[i] = i;\n    for (let i = 0; i < d; i++) {\n      const j = i + ((rnd() * (K - i)) | 0);\n      const tmp = idx[i]; idx[i] = idx[j]; idx[j] = tmp;\n      mask[idx[i] >>> 5] ^= 1 << (idx[i] & 31);\n    }\n    packets++;\n\n    // --- reduce against stored pivots\n    let w = MASKW - 1;\n    for (;;) {\n      while (w >= 0 && mask[w] === 0) w--;\n      if (w < 0) break;                                   // reduced to zero: dependent\n      const bit = 31 - Math.clz32(mask[w]);\n      const p = (w << 5) | bit;\n\n      const pm = pivMask[p];\n      if (pm === null) {                                  // new pivot\n        pivMask[p] = mask.slice();\n        pivPay[p] = pay.slice();\n        rank++;\n        break;\n      }\n      const pp = pivPay[p];\n      for (let i = 0; i <= w; i++) mask[i] ^= pm[i];\n      for (let i = 0; i < PAYW; i++) pay[i] ^= pp[i];\n      rowOps++;\n    }\n  }\n\n  const ms = performance.now() - t0;\n  const bytes = rowOps * ROWB;\n  return {\n    K, L, cap, packets, rowOps, ms,\n    overheadPct: ((packets - K) / K) * 100,\n    xorBytes: bytes,\n    throughputMBs: bytes / 1e6 / (ms / 1000),\n    blockBytes: K * L,\n    matrixBytes: (K * K) / 8,\n  };\n}\n\n/** Sustained XOR the decoder must achieve to keep pace — matches ge_cost_model.py. */\nexport const requiredMBs = (K, L, wireBytesPerSec) =>\n  (K * (K / 8 + L) * wireBytesPerSec) / L / 1e6;\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------- report\nconst STAGES = [[\"Stage 1\", 30], [\"Stage 2\", 60], [\"Stage 3\", 106]];\nconst BUDGET = 200;                  // MB/s — the plan's assumed phone-JS figure\nconst PHONE_FACTOR = 4;              // desktop → mid-range phone, conservative\n\nfunction report(K, L) {\n  // warm V8, then take the best of 3 (we want the achievable ceiling, not GC noise)\n  run(K, L);\n  let best = null;\n  for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {\n    const r = run(K, L, { seed: 0xC0FFEE + i });\n    if (!best || r.throughputMBs > best.throughputMBs) best = r;\n  }\n\n  console.log(`\\nK=${best.K}  L=${best.L}  cap=${best.cap}`);\n  console.log(`  block ${(best.blockBytes / 1024).toFixed(0)} KB · matrix ${(best.matrixBytes / 1024).toFixed(0)} KB`);\n  console.log(`  packets ${best.packets} (overhead ${best.overheadPct >= 0 ? '+' : ''}${best.overheadPct.toFixed(2)}%) · row-ops ${best.rowOps.toLocaleString()}`);\n  console.log(`  decode ${best.ms.toFixed(0)} ms · XOR ${(best.xorBytes / 1e6).toFixed(0)} MB`);\n  console.log(`  THIS MACHINE: ${best.throughputMBs.toFixed(0)} MB/s`);\n  const phone = best.throughputMBs / PHONE_FACTOR;\n  console.log(`  est. phone (÷${PHONE_FACTOR}): ${phone.toFixed(0)} MB/s   [plan assumes ${BUDGET}]`);\n\n  for (const [name, kb] of STAGES) {\n    const need = requiredMBs(K, L, kb * 1024);\n    const ok = phone >= need;\n    console.log(`    ${name.padEnd(8)} needs ${need.toFixed(0).padStart(4)} MB/s  → ${ok ? 'OK' : 'FAILS'}  (${(phone / need).toFixed(2)}x margin)`);\n  }\n  return best;\n}\n\nif (typeof process !== 'undefined' && process.argv[1]?.endsWith('ge-bench.mjs')) {\n  const [, , kArg, lArg] = process.argv;\n  console.log('S1 — GE decoder throughput benchmark');\n  console.log('Validates the 200 MB/s phone-JS budget behind D19 (plan.md §18 R1).');\n  console.log(`Node ${process.version}`);\n  if (kArg) {\n    report(+kArg, +(lArg ?? 256));\n  } else {\n    for (const K of [512, 768, 1024, 1152]) report(K, 256);\n    console.log('\\nKill criteria (plan.md §17 Phase 0.5):');\n    console.log('  est. phone < required at Stage 3 → drop K, or re-open D5 vs wirehair (R1)');\n    console.log(`  Packet on the wire = ${HEADER} + L bytes.`);\n  }\n}\n","numLines":172,"startLine":1,"totalLines":172}}}

}\n27\t</style>\n28\t\n29\t<h1>GE Benchmark - Thermal Throttling Test</h1>\n30\t\n31\t<p>\n32\t  <strong>Purpose:</strong> Measure GE decode throughput while device thermally throttles.<br>\n33\t  <strong>Protocol:</strong> Run continuous decode for 30+ minutes, track performance degradation.<br>\n34\t  <strong>Throttling indicator:</strong> >30% throughput degradation from cool baseline.<br>\n35\t  <strong>Target:</strong> Validate ÷4 phone factor and measure sustained performance while throttled.\n36\t</p>\n37\t\n38\t<div class=\"controls\">\n39\t  <button id=\"startTest\">Start 30-min thermal test</button>\n40\t  <button id=\"stopTest\">Stop</button>\n41\t  <label>Target K: <input id=\"targetK\" type=\"number\" value=\"768\" min=\"256\" max=\"1152\"></label>\n42\t  <label>Duration (min): <input id=\"durationMin\" type=\"number\" value=\"30\" min=\"5\" max=\"120\"></label>\n43\t</div>\n44\t\n45\t<div id=\"status\" class=\"status\"></div>\n46\t\n47\t<div class=\"big\" id=\"summary\"></div>\n48\t\n49\t<div id=\"results\"></div>\n50\t\n51\t<script type=\"module\">\n52\timport { run, requiredMBs } from './ge-bench.mjs';\n53\t\n54\tconst STAGES = [[\"Stage 1\", 30], [\"Stage 2\", 60], [\"Stage 3\", 106]];\n55\tconst BUDGET = 200;                  // MB/s — the plan's assumed phone-JS figure\n56\tconst PHONE_FACTOR = 4;              // desktop → mid-range phone, conservative\n57\t\n58\tconst $ = (id) => document.getElementById(id);\n59\t\n60\tlet testInterval = null;\n61\tlet testData = [];\n62\tlet testStartTime = null;\n63\tlet isRunning = false;\n64\t\n65\tfunction setStatus(msg, cls = '') {\n66\t  const el = $('status');\n67\t  el.textContent = msg;\n68\t  el.className = 'status ' + cls;\n69\t}\n70\t\n71\tfunction addResult(text) {\n72\t  $('results').textContent += text + '\\n';\n73\t}\n74\t\n75\tfunction clearResults() {\n76\t  $('results').textContent = '';\n77\t  $('summary').textContent = '';\n78\t}\n79\t\n80\t// Detect throttling based on performance degradation\n81\tfunction detectThrottling(data, currentThroughput) {\n82\t  if (data.length < 5) return { throttled: false, reason: 'insufficient' };\n83\t\n84\t  // Use the first 5 measurements as baseline (cool state)\n85\t  const baseline = data.slice(0, 5);\n86\t  const avgBaseline = baseline.reduce((sum, d) => sum + d.throughput, 0) / baseline.length;\n87\t\n88\t  const degradation = ((avgBaseline - currentThroughput) / avgBaseline) * 100;\n89\t\n90\t  if (degradation > 30) {\n91\t    return { throttled: true, degradation: degradation.toFixed(1), reason: 'deg30' };\n92\t  } else if (degradation > 20) {\n93\t    return { throttled: false, degradation: degradation.toFixed(1), reason: 'deg20' };\n94\t  } else if (degradation > 10) {\n95\t    return { throttled: false, degradation: degradation.toFixed(1), reason: 'deg10' };\n96\t  }\n97\t\n98\t  return { throttled: false, degradation: degradation.toFixed(1), reason: 'stable' };\n99\t}\n100\t\n101\t// Run single benchmark iteration\n102\tasync function runSingleIteration(K, L) {\n103\t  try {\n104\t    const r = run(K, L, { seed: Math.floor(Math.random() * 1000000) });\n105\t    await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 10)); // Yield to UI\n106\t    return r;\n107\t  } catch (e) {\n108\t    console.error('Benchmark iteration failed:', e);\n109\t    return null;\n110\t  }\n111\t}\n112\t\n113\t// Start thermal test\n114\t$('startTest').onclick = async () => {\n115\t  if (isRunning) return;\n116\t\n117\t  const K = +$('targetK').value;\n118\t  const durationMin = +$('durationMin').value;\n119\t  const L = 256;\n120\t\n121\t  clearResults();\n122\t  isRunning = true;\n123\t  testStartTime = performance.now();\n124\t  testData = [];\n125\t\n126\t  setStatus('Starting thermal test...', 'running');\n127\t\n128\t  addResult(`Configuration: K=${K}, L=${L}, Duration: ${durationMin} min`);\n129\t  addResult(`Target: Measure GE throughput while device thermally throttles`);\n130\t  addResult(`Device info:`);\n131\t  addResult(`  UA: ${navigator.userAgent}`);\n132\t  addResult(`  Hardware concurrency: ${navigator.hardwareConcurrency || 'unknown'}`);\n133\t  addResult(`  Device memory: ${navigator.deviceMemory || 'unknown'} GB`);\n134\t  addResult(`  Platform: ${navigator.platform}`);\n135\t  addResult('');\n136\t\n137\t  addResult('Starting warmup iterations...');\n138\t\n139\t  // Warmup: run 5 iterations to establish baseline\n140\t  for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) {\n141\t    setStatus(`Warmup ${i+1}/5...`, 'running');\n142\t    const r = await runSingleIteration(K, L);\n143\t    if (r) {\n144\t      testData.push({\n145\t        elapsedMin: 0,\n146\t        throughput: r.throughputMBs,\n147\t        ms: r.ms,\n148\t        packets: r.packets,\n149\t        rowOps: r.rowOps,\n150\t      });\n151\t    }\n152\t    await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 100));\n153\t  }\n154\t\n155\t  addResult('Warmup complete. Beginning sustained test...\\n');\n156\t\n157\t  // Run continuous test for specified duration\n158\t  const intervalMs = 10000; // Run iteration every 10 seconds\n159\t  let iterationCount = 0;\n160\t\n161\t  testInterval = setInterval(async () => {\n162\t    if (!isRunning) {\n163\t      clearInterval(testInterval);\n164\t      return;\n165\t    }\n166\t\n167\t    iterationCount++;\n168\t    const elapsedSec = Math.floor((performance.now() - testStartTime) / 1000);\n169\t    const elapsedMin = (elapsedSec / 60).toFixed(1);\n170\t\n171\t    setStatus(`Running iteration ${iterationCount} (${elapsedMin} min)...`, 'running');\n172\t\n173\t    const r = await runSingleIteration(K, L);\n174\t    if (!r) {\n175\t      addResult(`[${elapsedMin} min] Iteration failed - skipping`);\n176\t      return;\n177\t    }\n178\t\n179\t    // Detect throttling\n180\t    const throttling = detectThrottling(testData, r.throughputMBs);\n181\t\n182\t    const entry = {\n183\t      elapsedMin: parseFloat(elapsedMin),\n184\t      elapsedSec,\n185\t      throughput: r.throughputMBs,\n186\t      ms: r.ms,\n187\t      packets: r.packets,\n188\t      rowOps: r.rowOps,\n189\t      throttled: throttling.throttled,\n190\t      degradation: throttling.degradation,\n191\t      reason: throttling.reason,\n192\t    };\n193\t\n194\t    testData.push(entry);\n195\t\n196\t    // Update results\n197\t    const phone = r.throughputMBs / PHONE_FACTOR;\n198\t    const statusClass = throttling.throttled ? 'throttled' : 'cool';\n199\t    const statusText = throttling.throttled ? '🔥 THROTTLED' : '✓ COOL';\n200\t\n201\t    const logEntry = `[${elapsedMin} min] ${statusText} ${r.throughputMBs.toFixed(0)} MB/s (device) → ${phone.toFixed(0)} MB/s (phone est)`;\n202\t    addResult(logEntry);\n203\t\n204\t    // Update summary\n205\t    const baseline = testData.slice(0, 5);\n206\t    const avgBaseline = baseline.reduce((sum, d) => sum + d.throughput, 0) / baseline.length;\n207\t    const current = r.throughputMBs;\n208\t    const change = ((current - avgBaseline) / avgBaseline * 100).toFixed(1);\n209\t\n210\t    $('summary').innerHTML = `\n211\t      Elapsed: ${elapsedMin} min | Current: <span class=\"${statusClass}\">${r.throughputMBs.toFixed(0)} MB/s (${change}% from baseline)</span><br>\n212\t      Phone est: ${phone.toFixed(0)} MB/s (target: 114.6 MB/s for Stage 3) | ${throttling.throttled ? '<span class=\"warn\">⚠️ THERMAL THROTTLING DETECTED</span>' : '<span class=\"ok\">✓ No throttling</span>'}\n213\t    `;\n214\t\n215\t    // Check if we've reached the target duration\n216\t    if (elapsedSec >= durationMin * 60) {\n217\t      clearInterval(testInterval);\n218\t      isRunning = false;\n219\t      completeTest(K, L);\n220\t    }\n221\t\n222\t  }, intervalMs);\n223\t};\n224\t\n225\t// Stop test\n226\t$('stopTest').onclick = () => {\n227\t  if (!isRunning) return;\n228\t\n229\t  clearInterval(testInterval);\n230\t  isRunning = false;\n231\t  setStatus('Test stopped by user', 'error');\n232\t  addResult('\\nTest stopped manually.');\n233\t  generateReport();\n234\t};\n235\t\n236\t// Complete test and generate report\n237\tfunction completeTest(K, L) {\n238\t  setStatus('Test complete - generating report...', 'done');\n239\t  addResult('\\n' + '='.repeat(60));\n240\t  addResult('TEST COMPLETE - GENERATING REPORT');\n241\t  addResult('='.repeat(60));\n242\t  generateReport();\n243\t}\n244\t\n245\t// Generate final report\n246\tfunction generateReport() {\n247\t  const K = +$('targetK').value;\n248\t  const L = 256;\n249\t\n250\t  addResult('\\n' + '='.repeat(60));\n251\t  addResult('THERMAL TEST REPORT');\n252\t  addResult('='.repeat(60));\n253\t\n254\t  // Calculate baseline (cool) and throttled performance\n255\t  const baseline = testData.slice(0, 5);\n256\t  const avgBaseline = baseline.reduce((sum, d) => sum + d.throughput, 0) / baseline.length;\n257\t\n258\t  // Find throttled samples (>30% degradation)\n259\t  const throttledSamples = testData.filter(d => {\n260\t    const degradation = ((avgBaseline - d.throughput) / avgBaseline) * 100;\n261\t    return degradation > 30;\n262\t  });\n263\t\n264\t  const avgThrottled = throttledSamples.length > 0\n265\t    ? throttledSamples.reduce((sum, d) => sum + d.throughput, 0) / throttledSamples.length\n266\t    : null;\n267\t\n268\t  addResult(`\\nConfiguration: K=${K}, L=${L}`);\n269\t  addResult(`Test duration: ${(testData[testData.length - 1].elapsedMin).toFixed(1)} minutes`);\n270\t  addResult(`Total iterations: ${testData.length}`);\n271\t\n272\t  addResult(`\\nCool performance (first 5 iterations):`);\n273\t  addResult(`  Device: ${avgBaseline.toFixed(0)} MB/s`);\n274\t  addResult(`  Phone est (÷${PHONE_FACTOR}): ${(avgBaseline / PHONE_FACTOR).toFixed(0)} MB/s`);\n275\t\n276\t  if (avgThrottled !== null) {\n277\t    addResult(`\\nThrottled performance (${throttledSamples.length} iterations >30% degradation):`);\n278\t    addResult(`  Device: ${avgThrottled.toFixed(0)} MB/s`);\n279\t    addResult(`  Phone est (÷${PHONE_FACTOR}): ${(avgThrottled / PHONE_FACTOR).toFixed(0)} MB/s`);\n280\t    addResult(`  Performance loss: ${((avgBaseline - avgThrottled) / avgBaseline * 100).toFixed(1)}%`);\n281\t  } else {\n282\t    addResult(`\\nNo thermal throttling detected during test.`);\n283\t    addResult(`Device maintained stable performance throughout.`);\n284\t  }\n285\t\n286\t  // Check against Stage 3 requirements\n287\t  addResult(`\\nStage 3 validation (106 KB/s wire rate):`);\n288\t  const need = requiredMBs(K, L, 106 * 1024);\n289\t\n290\t  if (avgThrottled !== null) {\n291\t    const phoneThrottled = avgThrottled / PHONE_FACTOR;\n292\t    const passes = phoneThrottled >= need;\n293\t    addResult(`  Required: ${need.toFixed(0)} MB/s`);\n294\t    addResult(`  Measured (throttled): ${phoneThrottled.toFixed(0)} MB/s`);\n295\t    addResult(`  Result: ${passes ? '✓ PASS' : '✗ FAIL'} (${(phoneThrottled / need).toFixed(2)}x margin)`);\n296\t\n297\t    if (!passes) {\n298\t      addResult(`\\n⚠️ WARNING: Throttled performance fails Stage 3 requirement!`);\n299\t      addResult(`   This device cannot sustain K=${K} under thermal load.`);\n300\t      addResult(`   Consider reducing K or implementing duty cycling (D27).`);\n301\t    }\n302\t  } else {\n303\t    const phoneCool = avgBaseline / PHONE_FACTOR;\n304\t    const passes = phoneCool >= need;\n305\t    addResult(`  Required: ${need.toFixed(0)} MB/s`);\n306\t    addResult(`  Measured (cool): ${phoneCool.toFixed(0)} MB/s`);\n307\t    addResult(`  Result: ${passes ?                  // MB/s — the plan's assumed phone-JS figure\nconst PHONE_FACTOR = 4;              // desktop → mid-range phone, conservative\n\nconst $ = (id) => document.getElementById(id);\n\nlet testInterval = null;\nlet testData = [];\nlet testStartTime = null;\nlet isRunning = false;\n\nfunction setStatus(msg, cls = '') {\n  const el = $('status');\n  el.textContent = msg;\n  el.className = 'status ' + cls;\n}\n\nfunction addResult(text) {\n  $('results').textContent += text + '\\n';\n}\n\nfunction clearResults() {\n  $('results').textContent = '';\n  $('summary').textContent = '';\n}\n\n// Detect throttling based on performance degradation\nfunction detectThrottling(data, currentThroughput) {\n  if (data.length < 5) return { throttled: false, reason: 'insufficient' };\n\n  // Use the first 5 measurements as baseline (cool state)\n  const baseline = data.slice(0, 5);\n  const avgBaseline = baseline.reduce((sum, d) => sum + d.throughput, 0) / baseline.length;\n\n  const degradation = ((avgBaseline - currentThroughput) / avgBaseline) * 100;\n\n  if (degradation > 30) {\n    return { throttled: true, degradation: degradation.toFixed(1), reason: 'deg30' };\n  } else if (degradation > 20) {\n    return { throttled: false, degradation: degradation.toFixed(1), reason: 'deg20' };\n  } else if (degradation > 10) {\n    return { throttled: false, degradation: degradation.toFixed(1), reason: 'deg10' };\n  }\n\n  return { throttled: false, degradation: degradation.toFixed(1), reason: 'stable' };\n}\n\n// Run single benchmark iteration\nasync function runSingleIteration(K, L) {\n  try {\n    const r = run(K, L, { seed: Math.floor(Math.random() * 1000000) });\n    await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 10)); Beginning sustained test...\\n');\n\n  // Run continuous test for specified duration\n  const intervalMs = 10000; // Run iteration every 10 seconds\n  let iterationCount = 0;\n\n  testInterval = setInterval(async () => {\n    if (!isRunning) {\n      clearInterval(testInterval);\n      return;\n    }\n\n    iterationCount++;\n    const elapsedSec = Math.floor((performance.now() - testStartTime) / 1000);\n    const elapsedMin = (elapsedSec / 60).toFixed(1);\n\n    setStatus(`Running iteration ${iterationCount} (${elapsedMin} min)...`, 'running');\n\n    const r = await runSingleIteration(K, L);\n    if (!r) {\n      addResult(`[${elapsedMin} min] Iteration failed - skipping`);\n      return;\n    }\n\n    // Detect throttling\n    const throttling = detectThrottling(testData, r.throughputMBs);\n\n    const entry = {\n      elapsedMin: parseFloat(elapsedMin),\n      elapsedSec,\n      throughput: r.throughputMBs,\n      ms: r.ms,\n      packets: r.packets,\n      rowOps: r.rowOps,\n      throttled: throttling.throttled,\n      degradation: throttling.degradation,\n      reason: throttling.reason,\n    };\n\n    testData.push(entry);\n\n    // Update results\n    const phone = r.throughputMBs / PHONE_FACTOR;\n    const statusClass = throttling.throttled ? 'throttled' : 'cool';\n    const statusText = throttling.throttled ? '🔥 THROTTLED' : '✓ COOL';\n\n    const logEntry = `[${elapsedMin} min] ${statusText} ${r.throughputMBs.toFixed(0)} MB/s (device) → ${phone.toFixed(0)} MB/s (phone est)`;\n    addResult(logEntry);\n\n    // Update summary\n    const baseline = testData.slice(0, 5);\n    const avgBaseline = baseline.reduce((sum, d) => sum + d.throughput, 0) / baseline.length;\n    const current = r.throughputMBs;\n    const change = ((current - avgBaseline) / avgBaseline * 100).toFixed(1);\n\n    $('summary').innerHTML = `\n      Elapsed: ${elapsedMin} min | Current: <span class=\"${statusClass}\">${r.throughputMBs.toFixed(0)} MB/s (${change}% from baseline)</span><br>\n      Phone est: ${phone.toFixed(0)} MB/s (target: 114.6 MB/s for Stage 3) | ${throttling.throttled ? '<span class=\"warn\">⚠️ THERMAL THROTTLING DETECTED</span>' : '<span class=\"ok\">✓ No throttling</span>'}\n    `;\n\n    // Check if we've reached the target duration\n    if (elapsedSec >= durationMin * 60) {\n      clearInterval(testInterval);\n      isRunning = false;\n      completeTest(K, L);\n    }\n\n  }, intervalMs);\n};\n\n// Stop test\n$('stopTest').onclick = () => {\n  if (!isRunning) return;\n\n  clearInterval(testInterval);\n  isRunning = false;\n  setStatus('Test stopped by user', 'error');\n  addResult('\\nTest stopped manually.');\n  generateReport();\n};\n\n// Complete test and generate report\nfunction completeTest(K, L) {\n  setStatus('Test complete - generating report...', 'done');\n  addResult('\\n' + '='.repeat(60));\n  addResult('TEST COMPLETE - GENERATING REPORT');\n  addResult('='.repeat(60));\n  generateReport();\n}\n\n// Generate final report\nfunction generateReport() {\n  const K = +$('targetK').value;\n  const L = 256;\n\n  addResult('\\n' + '='.repeat(60));\n  addResult('THERMAL TEST REPORT');\n  addResult('='.repeat(60));\n\n  // Calculate baseline (cool) and throttled performance\n  const baseline = testData.slice(0, 5);\n  const avgBaseline = baseline.reduce((sum, d) => sum + d.throughput, 0) / baseline.length;\n\n  // Find throttled samples (>30% degradation)\n  const throttledSamples = testData.filter(d => {\n    const degradation = ((avgBaseline - d.throughput) / avgBaseline) * 100;\n    return degradation > 30;\n  });\n\n  const avgThrottled = throttledSamples.length > 0\n    ? throttledSamples.reduce((sum, d) => sum + d.throughput, 0) / throttledSamples.length\n    : null;\n\n  addResult(`\\nConfiguration: K=${K}, L=${L}`);\n  addResult(`Test duration: ${(testData[testData.length - 1].elapsedMin).toFixed(1)} minutes`);\n  addResult(`Total iterations: ${testData.length}`);\n\n  addResult(`\\nCool performance (first 5 iterations):`);\n  addResult(`  Device: ${avgBaseline.toFixed(0)} MB/s`);\n  addResult(`  Phone est (÷${PHONE_FACTOR}): ${(avgBaseline / PHONE_FACTOR).toFixed(0)} MB/s`);\n\n  if (avgThrottled !== null) {\n    addResult(`\\nThrottled performance (${throttledSamples.length} iterations >30% degradation):`);\n    addResult(`  Device: ${avgThrottled.toFixed(0)} MB/s`);\n    addResult(`  Phone est (÷${PHONE_FACTOR}): ${(avgThrottled / PHONE_FACTOR).toFixed(0)} MB/s`);\n    addResult(`  Performance loss: ${((avgBaseline - avgThrottled) / avgBaseline * 100).toFixed(1)}%`);\n  } else {\n    addResult(`\\nNo thermal throttling detected during test.`);\n    addResult(`Device maintained stable performance throughout.`);\n  }\n\n  // Check against Stage 3 requirements\n  addResult(`\\nStage 3 validation (106 KB/s wire rate):`);\n  const need = requiredMBs(K, L, 106 * 1024);\n\n  if (avgThrottled !== null) {\n    const phoneThrottled = avgThrottled / PHONE_FACTOR;\n    const passes = phoneThrottled >= need;\n    addResult(`  Required: ${need.toFixed(0)} MB/s`);\n    addResult(`  Measured (throttled): ${phoneThrottled.toFixed(0)} MB/s`);\n    addResult(`  Result: ${passes ? '✓ PASS' : '✗ FAIL'} (${(phoneThrottled / need).toFixed(2)}x margin)`);\n\n    if (!passes) {\n      addResult(`\\n⚠️ WARNING: Throttled performance fails Stage 3 requirement!`);\n      addResult(`   This device cannot sustain K=${K} under thermal load.`);\n      addResult(`   Consider reducing K or implementing duty cycling (D27).`);\n    }\n  } else {\n    const phoneCool = avgBaseline / PHONE_FACTOR;\n    const passes = phoneCool >= need;\n    addResult(`  Required: ${need.toFixed(0)} MB/s`);\n    addResult(`  Measured (cool): ${phoneCool.toFixed(0)} MB/s`);\n    addResult(`  Result: ${passes ? '✓ PASS' : '✗ FAIL'} (${(phoneCool / need).toFixed(2)}x margin)`);\n    addResult(`\\nNote: No throttling observed. Test on a warmer device or extend duration.`);\n  }\n\n  // Phone factor validation\n  addResult(`\\nPhone factor validation:`);\n  addResult(`  Desktop → Phone factor assumed: ÷${PHONE_FACTOR}`);\n  addResult(`  Measured device throughput: ${avgBaseline.toFixed(0)} MB/s`);\n  addResult(`  This factor is: ${avgBaseline / PHONE_FACTOR >= BUDGET ?
